# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

1 # Netlist grammar
2 
3 Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
4 Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
5 MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
6 Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
7 SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
8 ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
9 R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
10 Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
11 Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

PY
python3 - <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('dut.spice'); base=p.read_text()
for i,cap in enumerate(['2p','5p','10p'],8):
 p.write_text(base.replace('CCOMP CM G 2p',f'CCOMP CM G {cap}'))
 with open('REQUEST.json','w') as f: json.dump({'action':'measure','experiment_id':f'trial{i:03d}_nominal','analysis':'nominal','pvt':'N'},f)
 q=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
 try: d=json.loads(q.stdout)
 except: print(i,'ERROR',q.stdout,q.stderr); continue
 print('TRIAL',i,'Ccomp',cap,'ok',d.get('ok'),'budget',d.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'),flush=True)
 for c in d.get('cases',[]):
  m=c.get('measurements',{}); e=m.get('envelope',{}); print(c['case_id'],c.get('status'),round(m.get('vout_v',0),4),round(m.get('overhead_a',0)*1e6,1),'uA','env',round(e.get('min_v',0),4),round(e.get('max_v',0),4),'rec',[None if w.get('recovery_s') is None else round(w['recovery_s']*1e6,2) for w in m.get('recovery_windows',[])],flush=True)
 print('misses',[(t['case_id'],t['metric']) for t in d.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]) if t['verdict']"'!='"'PASS'],flush=True)
 with open('trials.jsonl','a') as f: f.write(json.dumps({'trial':i,'netlist':p.read_text(),'result':d})+'\\n')
PY"

MODEL

The step responses suggest the pass gate rises too slowly after load release. I’m replacing its fixed pull-up current with a PMOS driven by the amplifier, so both gate directions respond to the sensed error.

MODEL

The fully switched pull-up made light-load bursts worse despite excellent DC accuracy. I’m returning to the gentler bias and isolating the pass gate with a small resistor so the output-coupled capacitor can act directly at fast edges.

p=Path('dut.spice'); s=p.read_text().replace('XPPULL GD PB','XPPULL G PB').replace('XNDRV GD X','XNDRV G X').replace('RGATE GD G 10k\\n','').replace('CFAS G VOUT 15p','CFAS G VOUT 10p\\nCMIR VOUT MIR 2p');p.write_text(s)

python3 - <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('dut.spice'); base=p.read_text()
for i,cap in enumerate(['2p','5p','10p','20p'],26):
 s=base.replace('CMIR VOUT MIR 2p',f'CMIR VOUT MIR {cap}')
 p.write_text(s)
 with open('REQUEST.json','w') as f: json.dump({'action':'measure','experiment_id':f'trial{i:03d}_nominal','analysis':'nominal','pvt':'N'},f)
 q=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
 try: d=json.loads(q.stdout)
 except: print(i,'ERROR',q.stdout,q.stderr); continue
 print('TRIAL',i,'Cmir',cap,'ok',d.get('ok'),'budget',d.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'),flush=True)
 for c in d.get('cases',[]):
  m=c.get('measurements',{}); e=m.get('envelope',{}); print(c['case_id'],c.get('status'),round(m.get('vout_v',0),4),'env',round(e.get('min_v',0),4),round(e.get('max_v',0),4),'rec',[None if w.get('recovery_s') is None else round(w['recovery_s']*1e6,2) for w in m.get('recovery_windows',[])],flush=True)
 print('misses',[(t['case_id'],t['metric']) for t in d.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]) if t['verdict']"'!='"'PASS'],flush=True)
 with open('trials.jsonl','a') as f: f.write(json.dumps({'trial':i,'netlist':s,'result':d})+'\\n')
PY"

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
for pvt in ['SLC','SLH','FLC','FLH']:
 with open('REQUEST.json','w') as f: json.dump({'action':'measure','experiment_id':f'trial026_{pvt}_200p','analysis':'nominal','pvt':pvt},f)
 q=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)

 except: print(pvt,'ERROR',q.stdout,q.stderr); continue
 print('PVT',pvt,'ok',d.get('ok'),'budget',d.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'),flush=True)

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('dut.spice'); base=p.read_text().replace('CMIR VOUT MIR 3p','CMIR VOUT MIR 2p')
for i,(w,l) in enumerate([(1,0.5),(2,0.5),(1,0.15),(2,0.15)],36):
 s=base.replace('XPPULL G PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=5 l=0.15',f'XPPULL G PB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=5 l=0.15\\nXPAUX G X VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w={w} l={l}')
 p.write_text(s)
 with open('REQUEST.json','w') as f: json.dump({'action':'measure','experiment_id':f'trial{i:03d}_SLC','analysis':'nominal','pvt':'SLC'},f)
 q=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
 try: d=json.loads(q.stdout)
 except: print(i,'ERROR',q.stdout,q.stderr); continue

/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* Shift both comparison signals toward VIN for NMOS input headroom
RREFHI VIN RP 500k
RREFLO RP VREF 500k
RFBVIN VIN FM 500k
RFBOUT VOUT FM 1meg
RFBGND FM VSS 1meg
* Low-voltage NMOS current reference and tail
RNB VIN NB 50k
XNBIAS NB NB VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=5 l=0.15
XNTAIL TAIL NB VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=10 l=0.15
* NMOS differential pair with PMOS current mirror
XNREF PD RP TAIL VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=20 l=0.5
XNFB X FM TAIL VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=20 l=0.5
XPDIOD PD PD VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=10 l=0.5
XPMIR X PD VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=10 l=0.5
* Pass-gate drive and local fast output feedback
RGUP VIN G 50k
XNDRV G X VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=10 l=0.5
XPASS VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.15 m=4
CFAS G VOUT 10p
.ends DUT
EOF

 s=base.replace('XPMIR G PD VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=10 l=0.5',f'XPMIR G PD VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w={w} l=0.5')

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('dut.spice'); base=p.read_text()
settings=[(76,100,0.5,1,10,0.5),(77,100,0.5,1,20,1),(78,100,1,2,20,1),(79,100,0.5,1,40,2)]
for i,pw,pl,pm,nw,nl in settings:
 s=base
 for name in ['XPREF','XPFB']:
  old=f'{name} '
  lines=s.splitlines();lines=[(line.replace('w=30 l=0.15',f'w={pw} l={pl} m={pm}') if line.startswith(old) else line) for line in lines];s='\\n'.join(lines)+'\\n'
 for name in ['XNBIAS','XNMIR']:
  lines=s.splitlines();lines=[(line.replace('w=10 l=0.5',f'w={nw} l={nl}') if line.startswith(name+' ') else line) for line in lines];s='\\n'.join(lines)+'\\n'
 p.write_text(s)
 vals=[]
 for pvt in ['N','SLC','FLC','FHH']:
  with open('REQUEST.json','w') as f: json.dump({'action':'measure','experiment_id':f'trial{i:03d}_{pvt}_dchigh','analysis':'dc_high','pvt':pvt},f)
  q=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:d=json.loads(q.stdout)
  except: print(i,pvt,'ERROR',q.stdout,q.stderr); continue
  c=d.get('cases',[{}])[0];m=c.get('measurements',{}); vals.append((pvt,round(m.get('vout_v',0),4),round(m.get('overhead_a',0)*1e6,1)))
  with open('trials.jsonl','a') as f: f.write(json.dumps({'trial':i,'pvt':pvt,'netlist':s,'result':d})+'\\n')
 print('trial',i,'geom',pw,pl,pm,nw,nl,vals,flush=True)

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in Path('trials.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
for trial in [76,78]:
 s=next(d['netlist'] for d in rows if d.get('trial')==trial)
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(s)
 for pvt in ['N','SLC','FLC','FHH']:
  with open('REQUEST.json','w') as f: json.dump({'action':'measure','experiment_id':f'trial{trial:03d}_{pvt}_nominal','analysis':'nominal','pvt':pvt},f)
  q=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:d=json.loads(q.stdout)
  except: print(trial,pvt,'ERROR',q.stdout,q.stderr); continue
  print('TRIAL',trial,'PVT',pvt,'ok',d.get('ok'),'budget',d.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'),flush=True)
  for c in d.get('cases',[]):
   m=c.get('measurements',{});e=m.get('envelope',{}); print(c['case_id'],round(m.get('vout_v',0),4),round(m.get('overhead_a',0)*1e6,1),'uA','env',round(e.get('min_v',0),4),round(e.get('max_v',0),4),'rec',[None if w.get('recovery_s') is None else round(w['recovery_s']*1e6,2) for w in m.get('recovery_windows',[])],flush=True)
  print('misses',[(t['case_id'],t['metric']) for t in d.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]) if t['verdict']"'!='"'PASS'],flush=True)
  with open('trials.jsonl','a') as f: f.write(json.dumps({'trial':trial,'pvt':pvt,'analysis':'nominal','netlist':s,'result':d})+'\\n')

import json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
rows=[json.loads(l) for l in Path('trials.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
base=next(d['netlist'] for d in rows if d.get('trial')==88)
settings=[(90,'0.7p'),(91,'0.8p'),(92,'0.9p'),(93,'1.1p')]
for trial,cap in settings:
 s=base.replace('CMIR VOUT MIR 1p',f'CMIR VOUT MIR {cap}')
 Path('dut.spice').write_text(s)
 vals=[]
 for pvt,analysis in [('FLC','mid_heavy'),('N','burst'),('FHH','light_heavy'),('SHC','light_heavy'),('SLC','burst')]:
  with open('REQUEST.json','w') as f: json.dump({'action':'measure','experiment_id':f'trial{trial:03d}_{pvt}_{analysis}','analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt},f)
  q=subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request','REQUEST.json'],capture_output=True,text=True)
  try:d=json.loads(q.stdout)
  except:print(trial,pvt,'ERROR',q.stdout,q.stderr);continue
  c=d.get('cases',[{}])[0];m=c.get('measurements',{});e=m.get('envelope',{});vals.append((pvt,analysis,round(e.get('min_v',0),4),round(e.get('max_v',0),4),[None if w.get('recovery_s') is None else round(w['recovery_s']*1e6,2) for w in m.get('recovery_windows',[])],[(t['metric'],t['verdict']) for t in d.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]) if t['verdict']"'!='"'PASS']))
  with open('trials.jsonl','a') as f: f.write(json.dumps({'trial':trial,'pvt':pvt,'analysis':analysis,'netlist':s,'result':d})+'\\n')
 print('TRIAL',trial,'Cmir',cap,vals,flush=True)
